By training regression model on these trajectories, we can predict optimal mixtures at multiple training stages. RegMix-D supports two deployment modes: an offline variant that generates a complete mixture schedule before target training, and an online variant that adapts the mixture during training using observed loss. Experiments on 25B tokens of the Pile dataset with a 1B parameter target model show that RegMix-D consistently improves over RegMix and DoReMi across 13 downstream tasks while remaining proxy-efficient: it surpasses RegMix even with only 128 proxy models (25% of RegMix's proxy compute budget).

</details>


### [109] [TW-LegalBench: Measuring Taiwanese Legal Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18699)
*Fei-Yueh Chen,Chun Huang Lin,Chan Wei Hsu,Kuan Hsuan Yeh,Zih-Ching Chen,Kuan-Ming Chen,Patrick Chung-Chia Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 TW-LegalBench，用于评估大型语言模型在台湾法律体系下的法律推理、答题与判决预测能力；结果显示模型在部分资格考试上接近或超过律师通过门槛，但在更高标准与精确法条引用方面仍存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律评测多集中于英文普通法资料或简体中文民法法系资料，针对台湾法律体系的司法语料与专业考试场景评估不足，因此需要一个面向该法域、任务类型更全面的基准。

Method: 构建包含三类任务的 TW-LegalBench：16000 余道多选题、117 道开放式申论题及 14000 余个法律判决预测样本；评估 13 个大型语言模型，分别采用多选准确率、基于评分细则分解的 LLM-as-Judge 申论评分框架，以及量刑准确性和法条引用等指标。

Result: 最佳模型超过律师资格考试通过门槛，但仍低于法官与检察官考试标准；在判决预测中，模型对判决类型与刑期预测表现尚可，但难以准确引用具体法律条文。

Conclusion: LLM 在法律资格考试类任务上已接近部分人类专业水平，但在可靠法律文本生成、精确法条引用和高要求法律职业标准方面仍有显著挑战。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown impressive capabilities across diverse tasks, yet their performance on jurisdiction-specific legal reasoning remains underexplored. We present TW-LegalBench that utilizes Taiwanese legal system's rich official corpus open to the public to fill the gap in evaluating LLMs on Taiwanese law, among common-law benchmarks that focus on English sources and civil-law benchmarks focusing on sources of Simplified Chinese. TW-LegalBench comprises three task types: (1) over 16,000 multiple-choice questions (MCQs) across five years of official examinations in 18 professional domains; (2) 117 open-ended essay questions (OEQs) from examinations for legal professionals with official scoring rubrics; and (3) more than 14,000 legal judgment prediction (LJP) instances covering hundreds of crime categories. We evaluate 13 LLMs using accuracy for MCQs, a decomposed LLM-as-Judge framework based on the scoring rubric points for OEQs, and metrics for sentencing accuracy and statute citation for LJP. Our results reveal that top-performing models exceed the passing threshold for qualified lawyers (passing rate: 11%) but fall short of that for judges and prosecutors (passing rate: 1~2%). For LJP, while models demonstrate reasonable verdict type accuracy and sentence prediction capability, they struggle to cite exact legal articles. These findings highlight that reliable legal text generation remains challenging for LLMs, even though their performance on qualification examinations approaches human level.

</details>


### [110] [LLMs Struggle to Measure What Distinguishes Students of Different Proficiency Levels: A Study of Item Discrimination in Reading Comprehension Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18709)
*Han Chen,Ming Li,Chenguang Wang,Yijun Liang,Dawei Zhou,Hong jiao,Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估42个大语言模型在零样本条件下估计教育测评题目区分度的能力，发现其与人工校准结果的相关性较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 题目区分度是教育测量中的核心心理测量属性，用于衡量题目能否有效区分高能力与低能力学生。已有研究多关注LLM估计题目难度的能力，但LLM是否能捕捉题目区分度仍不清楚。

Method: 研究评估42个闭源与开源权重大语言模型，采用两种互补方法：一是直接区分度预测，即让模型根据题目内容直接估计区分度；二是基于作答反应的经典测验理论（CTT）校准，即将LLM答案视为合成学生反应并计算区分度分数。

Result: 直接预测与人工校准区分度的一致性较弱，最佳模型Spearman相关仅为0.152。基于反应的CTT校准信号更强但仍有限，全部persona合成作答者池的Spearman相关达到0.241。

Conclusion: 题目区分度仍是LLM心理测量评估中的开放挑战。当前LLM包含一定非随机的区分度相关信号，但尚不能可靠刻画测评题目如何区分真实人类学生。

Abstract: Item discrimination is a fundamental psychometric property of educational assessment, which measures whether an item meaningfully distinguishes students with higher proficiency from students with lower proficiency. While various existing works have explored whether large language models (LLMs) can estimate item difficulty, it remains unclear whether they can capture item discrimination. In this work, we evaluate 42 proprietary and open-weight LLMs in zero-shot settings using two complementary approaches: direct discrimination prediction, where models explicitly estimate an item's discrimination value from its content, and response-based Classical Test Theory (CTT) calibration, where LLM answers are treated as synthetic student responses to compute discrimination scores. Our results show that direct prediction yields weak alignment with human-calibrated discrimination: the best-performing model reaches only a Spearman correlation of 0.152. Response-based CTT calibration provides a stronger but still limited signal, with the all-persona synthetic respondent pool reaching a Spearman correlation of 0.241. These findings highlight item discrimination as an open challenge for LLM-based psychometric evaluation: current LLMs contain non-random discrimination-relevant signal, but they do not yet reliably capture how assessment items distinguish human students.

</details>


### [111] [Morpheus: A Morphology-Aware Neural Tokenizer and Word Embedder for Turkish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18717)
*Tolga Şakar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Morpheus：面向土耳其语的神经形态素边界模型，兼具无损可逆、形态感知分词与词向量生成能力，在可逆分词、形态对齐和若干词汇级任务上优于常见子词方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 土耳其语是黏着语，语义大量承载于词缀/形态素中；现有基于语料统计的子词分词器容易切碎语义词缀，且部分 WordPiece 或规则分析器无法保证编码后可还原原文，不适合生成任务。

Method: Morpheus 使用神经字符级边界预测，并通过可微 Poisson-binomial 动态规划在训练阶段将字符边界概率转换为软形态素成员关系，推理时输出精确分段；不做字符串归一化，从结构上保证 decode(encode(w))=w。同时，同一次前向传播还能产生结构化词嵌入。

Result: 在可逆分词器中，Morpheus 达到最低 bits-per-character 1.425；MorphScore macro-F1 为 0.61，约为常见子词方法约 0.32 的两倍；相比 64K 词表子词分词器节省约 19% GPU 显存。作为冻结词向量，在词汇检索 root-family MAP 0.85、同根验证 ROC-AUC 1.00 上优于 BGE-M3 和 BERTurk；但在 NER、格/数探测等上下文和屈折相关任务上不如更重的上下文编码器。

Conclusion: Morpheus 展示了形态感知且无损可逆的神经分词在黏着语处理中的优势，并能提供强词汇级表示；其局限在于表示空间偏向词根结构，对强上下文依赖任务仍需上下文编码器补足。

Abstract: Turkish is agglutinative: meaning is carried by morphemes, yet the subword tokenizers that drive modern language models split words by corpus statistics, fragmenting semantically loaded suffixes and -- in the case of WordPiece and rule-based analyzers -- failing to decode their output back to the original text. This paper presents \textbf{Morpheus}, a neural morpheme-boundary model for Turkish that is at once a lossless, morphology-aware tokenizer and a word-embedding producer. A differentiable Poisson-binomial dynamic program turns per-character boundary probabilities into soft morpheme memberships during training and exact segments at inference, with no string normalization, so $\mathrm{decode}(\mathrm{encode}(w)) = w$ holds by construction. Because the model is neural, the same forward pass that tokenizes also emits a structured word embedding. Among reversible tokenizers -- the only ones valid for generation -- Morpheus attains the lowest bits-per-character ($1.425$), roughly doubles the gold morphological alignment of the subword family (MorphScore macro-F1 $0.61$ vs.\ ${\sim}0.32$), and uses ${\sim}19\%$ less GPU memory than 64K-vocabulary subword tokenizers. As an embedder, frozen Morpheus vectors lead on lexical retrieval (root-family MAP $0.85$) and same-root verification (ROC-AUC $1.00$), surpassing the multilingual retriever BGE-M3 and BERTurk; on context- and inflection-dependent tasks (NER, case/number probing) the heavier contextual encoders remain ahead -- a trade-off we attribute to Morpheus's root-centric geometry. Code: https://github.com/lonewolf-rd/TurkishMorpheus; model: https://huggingface.co/lonewolflab/Morpheus-TR-50K; interactive demo: https://huggingface.co/spaces/lonewolflab/morpheus-tr-demo.

</details>


### [112] [LegalWorld: A Life-Cycle Interactive Environment for Legal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18728)
*Songhan Zuo,Shengbin Yue,Tao Chiang,Guanying Li,Yun Song,Xuanjing Huang,Zhongyu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 LegalWorld 和 LongJud-Bench，用于评估智能体在中国民事诉讼全生命周期中的连续决策与能力表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律基准多评估孤立子任务，法律智能体模拟器常从共享真实信息重新初始化场景，难以建模诉讼不同阶段之间的因果依赖；而真实民事诉讼中早期文书与策略会持续影响后续庭审等阶段。

Method: 构建 LegalWorld：一个将中国民事诉讼建模为五个阶段、七个子场景的生命周期交互环境，基于 75,309 对中国民事判决数据，并引入本地记忆、全局案件记忆和 Skill/Tool 库以保持案件跨阶段一致性；进一步构建 LongJud-Bench，对智能体在五个相互连接阶段的能力进行评估。

Result: 来自 217 名法律背景评估者的 18,992 条评分表明，LegalWorld 生成的轨迹在程序忠实性和角色一致性上得到确认；跨模型能力评估显示，不同模型在咨询、起草和庭审辩论等能力上存在显著差异，单一聚合分数难以揭示这些差异，且没有一个模型在所有环节均领先。

Conclusion: 该工作强调法律智能体评估应从孤立任务转向具有因果连续性的全生命周期评估；LegalWorld 与 LongJud-Bench 为检验法律智能体在复杂诉讼流程中的一致性、阶段适应性和综合能力提供了新的基础设施与基准。

Abstract: Civil litigation is inherently a life-cycle process: what a lawyer drafts on day one constrains what unfolds at trial months later. Yet existing legal benchmarks evaluate isolated subtasks, and prior legal-agent simulators reinitialize each scenario from shared ground truth, leaving cross-stage causal dependencies unmodeled. We present LegalWorld, a life-cycle interactive environment that models Chinese civil litigation as a causally connected state chain of five stages (seven sub-scenarios), grounded in 75,309 paired Chinese civil judgments. We pair it with reusable infrastructure (local memory, global case memory, a Skill/Tool library) that keeps each dispute consistent across its full life cycle. Building on this environment, we construct LongJud-Bench to evaluate agent capability across all five connected stages. 18,992 ratings from 217 legal-background evaluators confirm that LegalWorld trajectories are procedurally faithful and role-consistent; and a capability-level cross-model evaluation reveals sharp divergences that aggregate scores cannot expose, with no single backbone leading across consultation, drafting, and courtroom advocacy. Detailed resources will be released publicly.

</details>


### [113] [Output Vector Editing for Memorization Mitigation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18767)
*Ahmad Dawar Hakimi,Kaiwei Lei,Isabelle Augenstein,Hinrich Schütze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“输出向量编辑”来抑制大语言模型复现训练数据中的记忆序列，相比将神经元激活置零的方法，在多个模型上取得更高抑制率并保持较好局部性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可能记忆并复现训练数据片段，带来隐私、版权与安全风险。现有神经元级缓解方法通常把编辑等同于将神经元激活清零，但激活只决定神经元是否参与计算，真正写入残差流的是输出向量；由于特征叠加，一个输出向量可能承载多种信息，因此简单置零可能不充分或副作用较大。

Method: 作者提出一种受约束优化的权重编辑方法：先定位负责某个记忆续写的小规模 MLP 神经元集合，再在尽量小改动的约束下修改这些神经元的输出向量，使其在词表空间引入干扰项，从而重定向其对残差流的贡献，同时保持神经元激活不变。实验覆盖 SmolLM-360M、OLMo-1B、OLMo-7B、Llama2-7B，重点在 OLMo-7B 上系统挖掘 6831 条记忆序列，并比较不同编辑模式、零消融和注意力头消融。

Result: 在 OLMo-7B 上最高达到 87.9% 的记忆序列抑制率；在相同定位神经元上，相比零消融有 2.7 倍优势，说明效果主要来自输出向量编辑而非仅仅定位。四种编辑模式形成从强抑制到轻量重定向的谱系，集成可覆盖 96.5% 的记忆序列；推荐单模式配置达到 81.5%，且无灾难性局部性失败。约 14% 的序列无法通过仅编辑 MLP 解决；对高贡献注意力头消融可恢复其中 60–64%，尤其对从前缀复制 token 的续写更有效。跨四个模型中，编辑模式排序和成功-局部性权衡基本可迁移，成功率随模型规模而非模型家族提升。

Conclusion: 输出向量比激活置零更适合作为神经元级记忆抑制的编辑对象；MLP 输出向量编辑是有效且相对局部的主机制，注意力可作为处理 MLP 不可达案例的补充后备机制。研究揭示了记忆抑制的机制边界，并提供了跨模型可迁移的编辑策略。

Abstract: Large language models memorize and reproduce sequences from their training data, creating privacy, copyright, and security risks. Existing neuron-level mitigation methods equate editing with zeroing out neuron activations, but the activation only controls whether a neuron engages; the output vector is what writes to the residual stream and, through superposition, encodes multiple features. We propose output vector editing, a constrained-optimization weight edit that locates a small set of MLP neurons responsible for a memorized continuation and minimally modifies their output vectors to introduce a distractor in vocabulary space, redirecting their residual-stream contributions while leaving activations unchanged. Evaluating on four models from 360M to 7B parameters (SmolLM-360M, OLMo-1B, OLMo-7B, Llama2-7B), we center on OLMo-7B (whose open weights and pretraining corpus enable systematic mining) and mine 6831 memorized sequences, achieving up to 87.9% suppression. The 2.7$\times$ gap over zero ablation on the same located neurons shows the suppression comes from the output-vector edit, not localization alone. Four edit modes span a spectrum from aggressive suppression to minimal redirection; in ensemble they cover 96.5% of memorized sequences, while our recommended single-mode configuration reaches 81.5% with no catastrophic locality failures. We further identify a mechanistic boundary at ${\sim}14%$ of sequences unreachable by MLP-only editing; while these failures are not attention-driven overall, ablating the top contributing attention heads recovers 60--64% of them, with stronger recovery on continuations that copy tokens from the prefix, positioning attention as a complementary fallback rather than a primary mechanism. Edit mode ordering and the success-locality trade-off transfer across all four models, with success rates scaling with model size rather than family.

</details>


### [114] [Lost in a Single Vector: Improving Long-Document Retrieval with Chunk Evidence Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18781)
*Shanshan Lyu,Yiwei Wang,Yujun Cai,Jiafeng Guo,Shenghua Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出长文档稠密检索中，整篇文档编码会稀释关键短证据片段，提出训练无关的 DICE 方法，通过分块独立编码并聚合为单向量，显著提升长文档检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稠密检索采用一个查询向量匹配一个文档向量。对于长文档，决定相关性的短片段可能在整篇文档编码过程中被弱化，导致文档级表示无法体现最强证据。作者希望刻画并缓解这种“文档侧早期压缩”失败模式。

Method: 提出 Evidence Dilution Index（EDI）衡量同一黄金文档中，文档级表示相对于最强 chunk 级证据的下降程度；提出 DICE（Document Inference via Chunk Evidence），将文档切分为多个 chunk，使用冻结的编码模型分别编码，再聚合回单个文档向量，保持标准的一查询一文档检索接口，且无需训练。

Result: 在 LongEmbed 上，DICE 在四种 backbone 上均提升检索性能，尤其在超过 4k tokens 的长文本切片上提升最大。例如 Dream 数据中，Passkey >4k 从 30.0 提升到 90.0，Needle >4k 从 23.3 提升到 74.0；在 12,779 个过滤样本中，DICE 在 92.8% 的案例中比单向量基线获得更低 EDI。

Conclusion: 结果表明，文档级编码策略是提升长文档检索的有效且尚未充分探索的方向；通过分块证据保留并聚合，DICE 能在不改变检索接口、不额外训练的情况下缓解关键证据稀释问题。

Abstract: Dense retrieval ranks one query vector against one document vector. On long documents, this interface can fail when a short but decisive span is weakened during document encoding before ranking. We study this failure mode as document-side early compression and introduce the Evidence Dilution Index (EDI) to measure how far a document-level representation falls below the strongest chunk-level evidence within the same gold document. Guided by this view, we propose DICE (Document Inference via Chunk Evidence), a training-free document-side strategy that splits documents into chunks, encodes them independently with a frozen model, and aggregates them back into a single vector while preserving the standard one-query-one-document interface. On LongEmbed, DICE improves retrieval across four backbones, with the largest gains on slices beyond 4k tokens: for Dream, Passkey >4k rises from 30.0 to 90.0 and Needle >4k from 23.3 to 74.0. Across 12,779 filtered samples, DICE yields lower EDI than the single-vector baseline in 92.8% of cases. These results establish document-level encoding as a practical and underexplored lever for long-document retrieval.

</details>


### [115] [RedactionBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18782)
*Sean Brynjólfsson,Shashvat Jayakrishnan,Esha Sali,Diptanshu Purwar,Madhav Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 RedactionBench 和 R-Score，用于评估大语言模型在上下文相关的个人信息脱敏任务中的能力，结果显示该问题仍未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脱敏评测往往把实体抽取与隐私语义混为一谈，无法反映信息是否应被遮盖取决于持有者、用途和上下文这一事实。

Method: 作者基于情境完整性理论构建了包含 200 篇、覆盖 11 个领域的人工标注基准 RedactionBench，并提出字符级指标 R-Score，以弱化格式差异和语义等价遮盖方式带来的影响；同时评估 NER 模型、小型语言模型和具备工具调用能力的前沿模型，并开展用户评估。

Result: 实验比较了 35 个模型，发现上下文脱敏仍具挑战。用户评估显示，对强制脱敏和安全保留文本的一致率较高，分别为 89.4% 和 94.1%，但对上下文脱敏的一致率仅为 47.7%。

Conclusion: 上下文隐私判断具有显著主观性，传统精确匹配指标不足以评估脱敏质量；RedactionBench 和 R-Score 可作为未来隐私保护系统设计与标准化评测的基线。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly applied to sensitive domains that require redaction of personally identifiable information (PII). While redacting PII is a data cleaning prerequisite, existing benchmarks conflate extraction mechanics with privacy semantics. A public phone number is not equivalent to a phone number in a medical record. Whether information constitutes a violation depends heavily on who holds it, why, and in what context, fundamentally differentiating redaction from simple entity recognition. Grounded in contextual integrity, we introduce RedactionBench, a manually annotated benchmark comprising 200 diverse documents across 11 domains, mostly seeded from real-world sources. We also introduce R-Score, a novel character-level metric that treats semantically similar redactions equally and nullifies shallow formatting choices, such as varying masking styles for phone numbers. Evaluations across Named Entity Recognition models, entity extraction Small Language Models, and frontier models equipped with agentic tools demonstrate that contextual redaction remains an unsolved problem. A human evaluation with over 80 users on RedactionBench reveals a stark dichotomy in privacy perceptions. Annotators show consensus with target labels for mandatory redactions (89.4 percent) and safe text preservations (94.1 percent), but fail to agree on contextual redactions (47.7 percent). This variance demonstrates the subjective nature of contextual privacy and motivates R-Score, which decouples contextual ambiguity from strict precision. We compare 35 models across families and report their performance in redacting PII. Finally, we release RedactionBench to establish a baseline for future privacy-preserving systems, hoping to inspire efficient model design and standardized evaluations.

</details>


### [116] [Beyond Scalar Scores: Exploring LLM-based Metrics for Clinical Significance Evaluation in Radiology Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18797)
*Qingyu Lu,Ruochen Li,Liang Ding,Yufei Xia,Youxiang Zhu,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究生成式放射科报告评估中的临床显著性边界，发现LLM评估器普遍能识别真实临床错误，但会过度惩罚无害改写；作者训练轻量级可解释指标以改善这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动评估指标常将报告质量压缩为缺乏医学依据的单一分数，难以区分真正影响临床的错误与无害表述差异；而LLM虽具备医学知识，也难以稳定划定临床显著错误边界。

Method: 以ReEvalMed为测试平台，从“Discrimination”（检测真实临床错误）和“Robustness”（容忍不显著变体）两个维度评估8种LLM评估器在one-pass与two-pass设置下的表现；同时合成4000对报告，基于Qwen3-8B和MedGemma-4B训练轻量级、可解释的评估指标。

Result: 实验发现广泛存在discrimination bias：模型能有效检测错误，但也会过度惩罚无害改写。训练后的指标更准确地区分临床显著错误与无害变体，超过32B规模医学LLM，并接近闭源模型表现。two-pass设置未能稳定提升整体性能，主要是在Discrimination与Robustness之间做权衡。

Conclusion: 面向成本敏感部署，one-pass训练指标更具实践价值；two-pass推理更适合需要精细平衡错误识别与鲁棒性的场景。作者计划发布数据集和指标。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of generated radiology reports requires strict clinical accuracy, as omitted critical findings or mischaracterized radiographic observations can directly affect patient care. Existing metrics obscure this requirement by reducing report quality to a medically ungrounded scalar. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) possess rich medical knowledge, they likewise struggle to draw a reliable boundary between clinically significant errors and harmless variation. We study this boundary using ReEvalMed benchmark as testbed and evaluate metric-level clinical significance from detecting true clinical errors ("Discrimination") and tolerating insignificant variations ("Robustness"). Across 8 LLM evaluators under one-pass and two-pass settings, we identify a widespread discrimination bias: models effectively detect errors but also over-penalize harmless rephrasings. To mitigate this, we synthesize 4k report pairs and train lightweight interpretable metrics on Qwen3-8B and MedGemma-4B. Our trained metric sharpens the clinical significance boundary, surpassing 32B-scale medical LLMs and remaining competitive with proprietary models. Crucially, the more costly two-pass setting fails to consistently improve overall performance and mainly trades discrimination for robustness. These findings suggest one-pass trained metrics as the practical choice for cost-sensitive deployment, with two-pass inference reserved for settings where D-R balance is critical. We will release the dataset and metric.

</details>


### [117] [Beyond Reward Engineering: A Data Recipe for Long-Context Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18831)
*Xiaoyue Xu,Sikui Zhang,Xiaorong Wang,Xu Han,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种以数据为中心的长上下文推理训练配方：构建覆盖检索、多证据综合与推理的约1.4万样本数据集，并结合最小化的结果奖励GRPO训练，即可显著提升大模型长上下文推理与智能体任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理对作为自主智能体部署的大语言模型至关重要。现有强化学习方法多聚焦奖励设计，而高质量、多样化训练数据不足；作者希望验证仅通过更好的数据配方是否能有效提升长上下文能力。

Method: 从数据中心视角构建三类互补任务数据：检索、多证据综合、推理；整理8个数据集共约14K样本。训练上采用简洁的基于结果奖励的GRPO设置，在Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B、Qwen3-30B-A3B等模型上进行强化学习，并评估七个长上下文基准及智能体任务迁移效果。

Result: 在三个模型上，七个长上下文基准平均分别提升+7.2、+3.2、+6.4分，超过先前RL训练集效果。将该数据配方继续用于已智能体调优的模型后，GAIA提升+4.8分，BrowseComp提升+7.0分。

Conclusion: 高质量且任务互补的数据配方本身能够在简单RL框架下显著增强长上下文推理能力，并能迁移到智能体场景；该工作强调数据构建在长上下文RL训练中的核心作用。

Abstract: Long-context reasoning is an essential capability for large language models, particularly when they are deployed as autonomous agents that must reason over lengthy trajectories. Reinforcement learning (RL) has recently emerged as a dominant paradigm for improving this ability, yet existing work largely focuses on reward engineering while diverse training data remains scarce. We revisit this problem from a data-centric perspective and show that a simple yet effective data recipe alone, paired with a minimal outcome-based GRPO setup, suffices to substantially improve long-context reasoning. Our recipe targets three complementary task families -- retrieval, multi-evidence synthesis, and reasoning -- for which we construct and curate eight datasets totaling ~14K examples. Experiments on three models (Qwen3-4B/8B/30B-A3B) yield average gains of +7.2/+3.2/+6.4 points across seven long-context benchmarks, surpassing prior RL training sets. We further demonstrate that these gains transfer to agentic tasks, where continuing RL training on an agent-tuned model with our data recipe improves GAIA by +4.8 and BrowseComp by +7.0 points. We will release our datasets to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [118] [ScholarSum: Student-Teacher Abstractive Summarization via Knowledge Graph Reasoning and Reflective Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18850)
*Bohou Zhang,Xiaoyu Tao,Mingyue Cheng,Huijie Liu,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ScholarSum 是一种面向科学文献摘要的分层反思式图框架，旨在同时提升语言流畅性与事实忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学文献的生成式摘要需要兼顾语言流畅和事实准确；传统抽取式方法容易破坏篇章逻辑，而基于大语言模型的生成方法虽流畅但容易产生事实不一致。

Method: 该方法先将文档切分为语义一致单元，并组织成分层知识图，以多层社区结构捕获全局逻辑和宏观主题；随后由“学生”生成初稿，再通过细粒度证据检索进行改写；最后由“教师”式审阅器迭代检查不受支持的内容，并触发定向重检索与重写。

Result: 实验表明，ScholarSum 在摘要完整性和事实忠实性方面显著优于已有基线方法。

Conclusion: ScholarSum 通过分层图结构建模、证据驱动生成和迭代反思审阅，有效缓解科学摘要中流畅性与事实一致性难以兼顾的问题。

Abstract: Abstractive summarization plays a crucial role in enabling efficient understanding of scientific literature, yet it inherently demands both linguistic fluency and factual faithfulness. Existing approaches often fail to reconcile these two requirements. Extractive methods rely on rigid sentence splicing that disrupts macro-level logical coherence, while large language model (LLM)-based generative approaches, despite mastering linguistic fluency, exhibit limited factual consistency. In this work, we propose ScholarSum, a hierarchical reflective graph-based framework that emulates a student-teacher writing process for fluent and faithful scientific summarization. ScholarSum first organizes the document into a hierarchical knowledge graph by segmenting it into semantically coherent units, whose multi-layered community structure captures global logic and macro-level themes. Guided by this global structure, the student generates an initial draft, which is subsequently refined through fine-grained evidence retrieval. To ensure factual consistency, a teacher-like reviewer then iteratively examines the draft, identifies unsupported content, and prompts targeted re-retrieval and rewriting until the summary meets rigorous quality standards. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ScholarSum significantly outperforms previous baselines in terms of both completeness and faithfulness. Our code is available at https://github.com/Xiaoyu-Tao/ScholarSum.

</details>


### [119] [Aligning Implied Statements for Implicit Hate Speech Generalizability with Context-Bounded Semi-hard Negative Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18852)
*Wicaksono Leksono Muhamad,Yunita Sari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Classifying implicit hate speech remains a challenge, as intent is often masked through insinuation and context rather than explicit slurs. Prior supervised contrastive approaches improve in-domain detection but can overfit surface cues and struggle to transfer across datasets. We propose ImpSH, a triplet-based framework that aligns posts with implied statements when available and uses context-bounded semi-hard negatives to focus learning on near confusions. We also examine AugSH, which forms positives via data augmentation. In controlled evaluations on IHC, SBIC, and DynaHate with BERT and HateBERT, ImpSH is a viable alternative to standard supervised contrastive baselines and often improves cross-domain performance under matched preprocessing and tuning budgets. Representation analysis using alignment and uniformity indicates tighter positive pairs with balanced global spread, and qualitative nearest-neighbor case studies illustrate typical false negatives under domain shift. These results demonstrate that aligning posts with their implied statements via context-bounded mining provides a more stable, bijective-like mapping to related insinuations, overcoming the volatility inherent in traditional clustering-based representation learning.

</details>


### [120] [Approximate Structured Diffusion for Sequence Labelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18856)
*Nicolas Floquet,Joseph Le Roux,Nadi Tomeh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利用扩散机制训练可条件于完整标签序列的 CRF，以增强序列标注中的长程依赖建模能力，并在 POS 标注上实现 16.5% 的错误率降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络参数化的线性链 CRF 在序列标注任务中表现良好，但通常只建模有限决策跨度（如标签二元组），表达能力受限；当任务需要捕捉长距离标签依赖时，性能可能下降。

Method: 作者将扩散思想引入 CRF 训练，使 CRF 能够以整个标签序列为条件进行建模；由于训练过程中条件来自带噪标签序列，因此结合近似 CRF 推断来完成标签预测。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能够提升标签准确率；在词性标注（POS-tagging）任务中，相比基线取得了 16.5% 的错误率降低。

Conclusion: 扩散条件化的 CRF 能突破传统线性链 CRF 的有限跨度限制，更好地利用全局标签序列信息，从而改善需要长程依赖的序列标注性能。

Abstract: Sequence labelling, a core task of Natural Language Processing (NLP), consists in assigning each token of an input sentence a label.
  From a Machine Learning point of view, sequence labelling is often cast as a Linear-Chain Conditional Random Field (CRF) parametrised by a neural network.
  While this approach gives good empirical results, CRFs assume a finite decision span (eg label bigrams) which can limit their expressivity and hurt performance when long-range dependencies are required.
  We show we can leverage diffusion to train a CRF conditioned on an entire label sequence, with the caveat that the condition is on a noisy version of labels.
  We show experimentally that this method, in conjunction with approximate CRF inference, improves label accuracy with a 16.5% error reduction for POS-tagging.

</details>


### [121] [Efficient Financial Language Understanding via Distillation with Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18875)
*Wen-Fong,Huang,Edwin Simpson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向金融情感分析的高效知识蒸馏框架：用少量人工标注样本进行聚类选种子，再通过结构化少样本提示生成合成数据，将大型指令微调教师模型的能力迁移到小型学生模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型指令跟随模型在金融领域部署成本高；金融标注数据受保密性和专家标注成本限制，低资源场景下难以获得充足监督信号。因此需要一种低成本、少标注、可部署的小模型领域适配方案。

Method: 先收集并人工标注少量真实金融文本；对这些样本进行聚类；基于聚类结果选择更具代表性的种子样本；利用大型指令微调教师模型和结构化 few-shot prompting 生成合成训练数据；再用合成数据蒸馏训练紧凑学生模型，并在不同文本域上评估。

Result: 聚类式种子选择相比随机采样能生成更具代表性的合成数据，使小模型在极少监督下获得较强表现。在更复杂、噪声更高的文本域中，使用完整合成种子语料训练的小模型甚至超过教师模型；在正式文本上也保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了“聚类选种子 + 合成数据 + 知识蒸馏”可有效降低金融 NLP 领域适配的人力标注和部署成本，为低资源金融情感分析提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Large instruction-following models are powerful but costly to deploy, particularly in finance, where labelled data are limited by confidentiality and expert annotation cost. We present an efficient framework for financial sentiment analysis through distillation with synthetic data, transferring knowledge from a large instruction-tuned teacher to compact student models. The framework is designed for low-resource conditions, where a small set of real examples are collected and labelled by hand. The framework then clusters the examples and uses the clusters to select seeds for generating synthetic examples via structured few-shot prompting. Experiments show that clustering-based seed selection yields more representative synthetic data than random sampling, enabling compact models to achieve strong performance with minimal supervision. Notably, on a more complex and noisy text domain, the compact model trained on the complete synthetic-seed corpus even outperforms the teacher model, while remaining competitive on formal text. The framework provides a practical route toward resource-efficient domain adaptation in financial NLP with minimal human labelling effort.

</details>


### [122] [Improving Medical Communication using Rubric-Guided Counterfactual Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18889)
*Adrian Cosma,Nicoleta-Nina Basoc,Andrei Niculae,Cosmin Dumitrache,Emilian Radoi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由语言模型引导的反事实推荐流程，用于在不改变医疗内容的前提下优化文本远程医疗中的沟通表达，并提升患者正向反馈概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本远程医疗越来越依赖患者的轻量级反馈，但这些反馈更多反映患者感知到的沟通质量，而不一定代表医疗准确性。因此，需要一种方法识别可解释的沟通改进点，同时避免干扰医生的医学判断和内容。

Method: 作者构建了一个LM引导的反事实推荐管线，发现并细化语气、个性化、可操作性、完整性等可解释沟通特征，并结合医患交互元数据估计正向反馈概率。在推理阶段，系统搜索低成本的有序特征变化，推荐最小化的沟通修改；同时使用独立审计模型检验推荐收益是否能泛化到选择模型之外。

Result: 在多组交互中，推荐方案在独立审计模型下带来平均+6.41%的预测正向反馈概率提升，且93.31%的推荐具有非负收益。

Conclusion: 结果表明，小幅、可解释的沟通层面调整能够获得大部分预测反馈收益，同时保留医生对医学推理和最终措辞的控制权。

Abstract: Text-based telemedicine increasingly relies on lightweight patient feedback, however, such feedback primarily reflects perceived communication quality rather than medical accuracy. We introduce an LM-guided counterfactual recommendation pipeline that discovers and refines interpretable communication features such as tone, personalization, actionability and completeness in addressing patient concerns, without interfering with the medical content. These features are used together with patient-doctor interaction metadata to estimate positive feedback. At inference time, the system searches over low-cost ordinal feature changes and recommends minimal communication changes predicted to increase the probability of positive feedback, while independent auditor models test whether these gains generalize beyond the selection model. Across interactions, recommendations yield a mean +6.41% gain in predicted positive feedback probability under independent auditors, and are non-negative for 93.31% of recommendations. These results suggest that small, interpretable communication changes can capture most predicted gains while preserving the doctor's control over medical reasoning and final wording.

</details>


### [123] [Learning Robust Pair Confidence for Multimodal Emotion-Cause Pair Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18893)
*Zhuangzhuang Pan,Ning Dong,Yingna Su,Yan Xia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出RPCL，一个仅用于训练阶段的鲁棒配对置信度学习框架，用于提升多模态情绪-原因对抽取中的pair confidence可靠性，并在多个数据集上取得稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MECPE配对评分器多使用候选对上的pair-level交叉熵，将链接近似独立处理，导致竞争原因之间的相对置信度结构约束不足；金标准配对可能与困难负例置信度接近，或依赖偶然的非金标准上下文，从而产生pair-confidence brittleness。

Method: RPCL从两个方面塑造配对置信度：一是通过置信度差值的margin约束，将金标准配对与同一行内的困难负例分离；二是构造非金标准上下文话语表示被部分破坏的corrupted view，并对齐clean view与corrupted view下的配对预测。推理阶段不改变原有clean pair scorer和解码流程。

Result: 在ECF、MECAD和MEC4三个数据集上，RPCL在完整文本-音频-视频设置下相较匹配基线模型将三随机种子平均Pair F1提升2.58至2.83个百分点，并在所有三个数据集上提升平均Pair AUPRC。诊断分析显示金标准-负例置信度间隔更大，margin violation严重程度更低。

Conclusion: 显式约束和塑造配对置信度的相对几何结构，可有效缓解MECPE中的置信度脆弱性，是一种不增加推理复杂度的有效训练策略。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion-cause pair extraction (MECPE) requires reliable pair confidence over candidate pairs. Existing pair scorers commonly use pair-level cross entropy over valid candidates, which treats links mostly independently. This leaves the relative confidence geometry among competing causes under-constrained, allowing gold pairs to stay close to hard negatives or rely on incidental non-gold context. We study this vulnerability as pair-confidence brittleness and propose RPCL (Robust Pair Confidence Learning), a training-only framework for pair-confidence learning. RPCL encourages pair confidence to be both discriminative and stable: gold pairs are separated from row-wise hard negatives through a confidence-difference margin constraint, and clean pair predictions are aligned with predictions from a corrupted view where non-gold contextual utterance representations are partially corrupted. The original clean pair scorer and decoding pipeline are used unchanged at inference time. On ECF, MECAD, and MEC4, RPCL improves the three-seed mean Pair F1 over a matched base model by 2.58 to 2.83 percentage points in the full text-audio-video setting, and improves mean Pair AUPRC on all three datasets. Diagnostic analysis further shows larger gold-negative confidence gaps and lower margin-violation severity. These results suggest that explicitly shaping pair confidence is an effective training strategy for MECPE.

</details>


### [124] [SAGE: Stochastic Prompt Optimization via Agent-Guided Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18902)
*Ziyi Zhu,Luka Smyth,Saki Shinoda,Jinghong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 SPO，将自动提示优化视为黑盒随机搜索，并比较随机搜索、遗传算法与多智能体 SAGE；实验显示不同策略优劣取决于任务景观与错误类型，SAGE 在持续优化场景中取得稳健留存提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明“文本梯度”并不等同于真实梯度，因此提示优化不应简单类比可微优化；同时，AI 系统可通过上下文工程在不更新参数的情况下提升性能，亟需系统化的黑盒提示搜索框架。

Method: 作者提出 Stochastic Prompt Optimization（SPO）框架，在提示空间中进行随机搜索；比较三类策略：基于错误信息的随机搜索、带进化算子的遗传算法，以及结合多智能体诊断与代码执行的 SAGE。并在三个基准任务和一个面向任务型对话的持续 A/B 测试优化场景中评估。

Result: 三个基准上没有单一方法始终最优，策略效果取决于搜索景观结构与错误类型之间的交互；SAGE 在连续优化场景中，将八轮单独噪声较大的 A/B 测试累积为统计上稳健的次日留存提升。

Conclusion: 自动提示优化更适合被建模为黑盒随机搜索；在开放式任务型对话中，将定性诊断与定量验证结合，是智能体式提示优化有效性的关键。

Abstract: Context engineering has emerged as a primary lever for improving AI systems without parameter updates. Recent work showing that textual gradients do not function as real gradients motivates treating automatic prompt optimization (APO) as black-box search. We introduce SPO (Stochastic Prompt Optimization), a framework for stochastic search over prompt space, and compare three strategies of increasing sophistication: error-informed random search, a genetic algorithm with evolutionary operators, and SAGE (SPO via Agent-Guided Exploration), a multi-agent pipeline with diagnostic code execution. Across three benchmarks, no single strategy dominates; effectiveness depends on the interaction of landscape structure with error type. We further deploy SAGE on a mental-health chatbot under a continuous optimization paradigm, where it compounds eight cycles of individually-noisy A/B tests into a statistically robust gain in next-day retention. We argue that coupling qualitative diagnosis with quantitative validation is what makes agentic optimization effective for open-ended task-oriented dialogue.

</details>


### [125] [As Easy as Rocket Science: Assessing the Ability of Large Language Models to Interpret Negation in Figurative Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18922)
*Jasmine Owers,Edwin Simpson,Martha Lewis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大型语言模型在同时包含否定与比喻语言的文本理解任务中的表现，发现二者叠加会显著增加理解难度，且结果强烈依赖提示词风格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比喻语言和否定都是当前语言模型理解中的难点，并且在日常书面与口语表达中非常常见。由于大型语言模型常在无法针对特定数据集微调的实际场景中使用，因此有必要系统评估其对同时包含否定和比喻语言文本的解释能力。

Method: 研究者在一个已有的比喻语言数据集上新增否定相关标注，并使用该数据集测试多种语言模型。实验重点考察模型在不同否定类型、是否包含比喻表达以及不同提示词风格下的表现差异。

Result: 实验发现，否定与比喻性的组合会给模型带来特别大的挑战。模型总体性能以及在不同否定类型上的表现均明显受到提示词风格影响。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型在处理否定与比喻语言交互时仍存在不稳定和脆弱性。提示设计对性能有关键影响，未来需要更稳健的评测数据、提示策略以及模型改进方法来提升此类复杂语言现象的理解能力。

Abstract: Figurative language and negation are two areas that challenge current language models, however, both are widely used throughout written and spoken language. Large language models (LLMs) are also widely used in everyday contexts where they cannot necessarily be tuned for a specific dataset. It is therefore essential to understand the ability of LLMs to correctly interpret text that includes both negation and figurative language. To investigate this, we develop a set of new annotations to an existing dataset of figurative language, and test a range of language models on the dataset. We find that the combination of negation and figurativeness can present a particular challenge, and that performance overall and across different negation types is particularly dependent on the prompt style used.

</details>


### [126] [SenFlow: Inter-Sentence Flow Modeling for AI-Generated Text Detection in Hybrid Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18946)
*Jingkun Luo,Yifan Sun,Da-Tian Peng,Guanxiong Pei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出面向混合文档的句子级AI生成文本检测基准MOSAIC与模型SenFlow，将任务建模为文档句子序列上的结构化预测，并在跨域迁移等设置下取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有句子级AI生成文本检测方法通常孤立判断每个句子，忽略句间依赖；同时既有基准未覆盖更新一代生成器，难以评估真实混合写作场景中的检测能力。

Method: 构建包含16,000篇PubMed与XSum混合文档的MOSAIC基准，由DeepSeek-V3.2和Kimi K2生成，并加入严格质量控制与困惑度一致性过滤。提出SenFlow，将S-AGTD重述为文档句子序列的结构化预测，结合基于图的句间信息传播与线性链CRF解码，在一次文档级句子图前向过程中完成检测。

Result: SenFlow在MOSAIC上达到当前最佳表现，尤其在最困难的跨域迁移协议中，平均Macro-F1较对比方法提升4.15个百分点。分析还发现，即使困惑度过滤削弱显性线索，AI插入句仍存在与生成器相关的句长差异，检测器会继续利用该线索。

Conclusion: 该工作表明句间依赖建模对混合文档中的句子级AI文本检测至关重要，同时新基准揭示了现有检测方法仍可能依赖句长等浅层生成器偏差，提示未来需发展更稳健、更少依赖表面特征的检测方法。

Abstract: Sentence-level AI-generated text detection (S-AGTD) for hybrid documents, where humans and LLMs co-author one text, faces two gaps: existing methods classify each sentence in isolation, discarding inter-sentence dependencies, and existing benchmarks omit the newest generation of generators. We construct MOSAIC, a benchmark of 16,000 hybrid documents over PubMed and XSum, generated by DeepSeek-V3.2 and Kimi K2 under stringent quality controls including a perplexity-consistency filter absent from prior benchmarks. We recast S-AGTD as structured prediction over the document sentence sequence and instantiate it as SenFlow, integrating graph-based inter-sentence propagation with linear-chain CRF decoding in a single document-level pass over a sentence graph. SenFlow reaches state-of-the-art performance on MOSAIC, with a +4.15 pp average Macro-F1 margin on cross-domain transfer, the hardest of three protocols of increasing difficulty. We further find that even after the perplexity filter equalizes overt cues, AI insertions retain a generator-dependent sentence-length gap that sentence-level detectors still exploit. Code and data: https://github.com/luojingkun22/SenFlow

</details>


### [127] [GraphPO: Graph-based Policy Optimization for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18954)
*Yuliang Zhan,Xinyu Tang,Jian Li,Dandan Zheng,Weilong Chai,Jingdong Chen,Jun Zhou,Ge Wu,Wenyue Tang,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GraphPO 将推理轨迹建模为有向无环图，合并语义等价状态以减少冗余探索，并通过边级优势分配提升效率与监督信号质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RLVR 通常独立采样响应，仅依赖最终答案奖励，导致中间推理重复、计算浪费以及奖励稀疏；树结构方法虽能共享前缀并提供局部比较，但无法在不同分支到达相似推理状态时共享信息，且可能增加优势估计方差。

Method: 提出 GraphPO：用有向无环图表示 rollout，将推理步骤作为边、由推理路径总结出的语义状态作为节点；把语义等价推理路径合并为等价类，共享后缀并将预算从冗余扩展转向多样化探索；同时为入边分配效率优势、为出边分配正确性优势，从结果奖励中推导过程监督。

Result: 理论分析表明 GraphPO 可降低优势估计方差并提升推理效率；在三个大语言模型以及推理和智能体搜索基准上的实验显示，在相同 token 预算或响应预算下，GraphPO 持续优于链式和树式基线。

Conclusion: GraphPO 通过图结构的状态合并与边级优势设计，缓解 RLVR 中冗余探索和稀疏奖励问题，是一种比链式/树式采样更高效、更稳定的策略优化框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become a standard paradigm for enhancing the capability of large reasoning models. RLVR typically samples responses independently and optimizes the policy using from final answers. This paradigm has two limitations. First, independently responses often contain similar intermediate reasoning steps, causing redundant exploration and wasted computation. Second, sparse final-answer rewards make it hard to identify useful steps. Tree-based methods partly address this problem by sharing prefixes and comparing branches from the same prefix to provide fine-grained signals. However, tree branches are still expanded independently. When different branches reach similar reasoning states, they cannot share information and repeat similar exploration. Moreover, tree-based methods ignore such dispersion and only perform local comparisons within separate branches, which can lead to higher variance in advantage estimation. To address this challenge, we propose GraphPO (Graph-based Policy Optimization), a novel RL framework that represents rollouts as a directed acyclic graph, with reasoning steps as edges and semantic states summarized from the reasoning paths as nodes. GraphPO merges semantically equivalent reasoning paths into equivalence classes, allowing them to share suffixes and reallocating budget away from redundant expansions to diverse exploration. Furthermore, we assign efficiency advantages to incoming edges and correctness advantages to outgoing edges, thereby improving inference efficiency while deriving process supervision from outcome. Theory shows that GraphPO reduces advantage-estimation variance and enhances reasoning efficiency. Experiments on three LLMs across reasoning and agentic search benchmarks show that GraphPO consistently outperforms chain- and tree-based baselines with the same token budgets or response budgets.

</details>


### [128] [Beyond Tokenization: Direct Timestep Embedding and Contrastive Alignment for Time-Series Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18986)
*Yafeng Wu,Huu Hiep Nguyen,Thin Nguyen,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 CADE 框架，将时间序列逐时间步直接嵌入到 LLM 表征空间，并通过监督对比学习与文本语义锚点对齐，以提升时间序列问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TSQA 方法直接将原始数值输入 LLM 会受到 BPE 分词瓶颈影响，导致幅值、尺度与趋势信息丢失；而基于 patch 的编码器固定粒度、可能破坏局部模式、隐藏精确时间步，并且跨数据集迁移性较差。

Method: CADE 包含两个核心组件：一是 direct timestep embedding，通过点级线性编码器和 MLP projector 将每个时间步直接映射到 LLM embedding 空间，保留索引级访问并避免 patching/padding；二是 semantic alignment，使用单向监督对比损失，将时间序列嵌入与冻结的类别名称文本锚点进行语义对齐。

Result: 在公开 Time-MQA benchmark 上，CADE 在六类 TSQA 任务中持续提升表现，并优于开源与闭源/专有 LLM 基线。

Conclusion: CADE 通过直接时间步嵌入和文本语义锚点对齐缓解了数值序列与语言模型之间的表示鸿沟，为 TSQA 提供了更细粒度、更可迁移的建模方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have given rise to time-series question answering (TSQA), which formulates time-series analysis as natural-language question answering. However, directly feeding raw numerical series into LLMs suffers from a tokenization bottleneck: Byte Pair Encoding fragments continuous values into unstable tokens whose embeddings lack meaningful metric structure, resulting in the loss of magnitude, scale, and trend information. Prior methods use patch-based encoders that split the series into fixed windows, locking in one granularity that breaks patterns and hides exact timesteps, through a separate module that rarely transfers across datasets with different lengths or sampling rates. To address this challenge, we propose CADE (Contrastive Alignment with Direct Embedding), a novel framework for TSQA built upon two key components: direct timestep embedding and semantic alignment. The proposed framework maps each timestep directly into the LLM embedding space through a point-wise linear encoder and MLP projector, preserving exact index-level access while eliminating the need for patching and padding. To further bridge the semantic gap between time-series and language representations, we introduce a novel one-directional supervised contrastive loss that aligns time-series embeddings with frozen class-name text anchors. Experimental results on the public Time-MQA benchmark demonstrate that our framework consistently improves performance across six TSQA tasks, outperforming both open-source and proprietary LLM baselines.

</details>


### [129] [G-IdiomAlign: A Gloss-Pivoted Benchmark for Cross-Lingual Idiom Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18989)
*Fengying Ye,Yanming Sun,Runzhe Zhan,Zheqi Zhang,Lidia S. Chao,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 G-IdiomAlign，一个以英文释义为语义枢轴的跨语言习语对齐基准，用于评估大模型在习语等价判断与生成中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 习语具有非组合性且表层形式与真实含义弱相关，跨语言迁移时字面翻译往往不可靠，尤其在低资源语言中更容易导致错误。因此需要一个可复现、能区分语义理解与字面映射偏差的评测基准。

Method: 作者基于 Wiktionary 为每个习语绑定英文 gloss，并构建高置信参考对齐集。基准包含两类协议：多项选择式习语等价任务，使用类型化干扰项支持错误归因；以及 gloss 对比生成任务，对比无 gloss 与有 gloss 输入，以隔离显式语义枢轴的作用。还对 Qwen3-8B 进行注意力层面分析。

Result: 多种 LLM 普遍存在字面翻译偏差，低资源目标语言中更明显。加入 gloss 后，在基于嵌入的语义代理指标下生成质量稳定提升，但总体性能仍有限。Qwen3-8B 的分析显示，不同条件下的差异更多集中在注意力头而非层级；更好的 With-gloss 生成与更强的 gloss anchoring 相关。

Conclusion: G-IdiomAlign 为跨语言习语理解与生成提供了可复现评测框架，证明显式语义释义有助于缓解字面翻译偏差，但当前 LLM 在开放式习语生成上仍有明显提升空间。

Abstract: Idioms are difficult to transfer across languages due to their non-compositionality and weak surface-form grounding, making literal mappings unreliable. We present G-IdiomAlign, a gloss-pivoted benchmark where each idiom is anchored by an English gloss from Wiktionary. We further construct a high-confidence reference alignment set for reproducible evaluation. G-IdiomAlign supports two protocols: (1) a controlled Multiple-Choice Idiom Equivalence with typed distractors for error attribution; and (2) a Gloss-Contrastive Generation contrasting No-gloss and With-gloss inputs to isolate the effect of an explicit semantic pivot. Across diverse LLMs, a bias to literal translation is a dominant failure mode, especially when the target is a low-resource language. Glosses consistently improve Gloss-Contrastive Generation under an embedding-based semantic proxy, but performance remains modest, indicating substantial headroom in the open output space. Subsequent analysis on Qwen3-8B further suggests that cross-condition differences are concentrated more in attention heads than in layers, while better With-gloss generations coincide with stronger gloss anchoring.

</details>


### [130] [Enhancing Multilingual Reasoning via Steerable Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19002)
*Zhuoran Li,Rui Xu,Jian Yang,Junnan Liu,Zhijun Chen,Qianren Mao,Hongcheng Guo,Jiaheng Liu,Likang Xiao,Ming Li,Xiaojie Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 ST-Merge，通过可控的模型合并机制自适应调节多语言模型与推理模型的贡献，从而提升多语言推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型合并能组合多语言能力与推理能力，但单一固定合并策略难以处理源模型之间的能力冲突；不同输入可能需要更依赖某个源模型，因此需要一种按输入动态调整模型贡献的方法。

Method: 提出 Steerable Model Merging（ST-Merge）框架，引入门控交叉注意力机制，对两个源模型的注意信息进行自适应加权或过滤，从而动态调制各源模型在合并模型中的贡献。

Result: 在四个多语言推理基准、覆盖 21 种语言的实验中，ST-Merge 持续优于多个强基线方法。

Conclusion: ST-Merge 证明了可控、输入自适应的模型合并比一刀切式合并更适合多语言推理场景，能够更有效缓解源模型冲突并提升泛化表现。

Abstract: Model merging is an effective technique for composing the capabilities of a multilingual model and a reasoning model. It has achieved promising generalization in multilingual reasoning tasks by aligning feature spaces of different models. However, the merged single model often fails to address the conflicts between source models, leading to suboptimal performance. In other words, the one-size-fits-all merging strategy may not align with the characteristics of different inputs which may require prioritizing certain models over others. To this end, we propose a Steerable Model Merging (ST-Merge) framework to modulate the contribution of each source model. To realize this idea, we introduce a gated cross-attention mechanism to weight or filter the two attended source models in an adaptive manner. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ST-Merge consistently outperforms multiple strong baselines on four multilingual reasoning benchmarks across 21 different languages.

</details>


### [131] [Sumi: Open Uniform Diffusion Language Model from Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19005)
*Mengyu Ye,Keito Kudo,Wataru Ikeda,Ryosuke Matsuda,Keisuke Sakaguchi,Jun Suzuki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍 Sumi：一个从零开始、以 1.5T tokens 预训练的完全开放 7B 统一扩散语言模型，为大规模 UDLM 研究提供基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一扩散语言模型允许任意 token 在任意步骤更新，具备更灵活的生成潜力；但此前缺乏同时具备大参数规模和大 token 预算、从零预训练的开放 UDLM，限制了社区对其扩展规律、生成动态、可控性及与自回归/掩码扩散模型权衡的系统研究。

Method: 作者提出 Sumi，一个 7B 参数的 uniform diffusion language model，从零在 1.5T tokens 上预训练，并公开模型权重、训练检查点、完整训练方案，以及基于公开语料的数据混合配方。

Result: Sumi 在知识、推理和代码基准上相较于相近 token 预算训练的自回归模型具有竞争力；但在常识类基准上表现较弱，作者推测与偏教育类的数据混合有关。

Conclusion: Sumi 提供了大规模原生统一扩散语言模型的开放参考点，有助于社区研究 UDLM 的扩展行为、生成机制和实际应用权衡，并推动该方向进一步发展。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become a promising alternative to autoregressive models. Among these, uniform diffusion language models (UDLMs) permit any token to be updated at any step, in principle enabling more flexible generation. However, no UDLM has yet been pretrained from scratch at both large parameter scale and large token budget. Both autoregressive modeling and masked diffusion modeling already have capable models at scale that the community can study and build on; uniform diffusion has none. A scratch-pretrained UDLM at scale would provide a clean reference point for studying scaling behavior, generation dynamics, controllability, and trade-offs against established autoregressive and masked diffusion models. To this end, we introduce Sumi ("ink" in Japanese), a fully open 7B uniform diffusion language model pretrained from scratch on 1.5T tokens. Sumi performs competitively with autoregressive models trained at comparable token budgets on knowledge, reasoning, and coding benchmarks, while under-performing on commonsense benchmarks, where our education-heavy data mixture is a likely contributor. We release our model weights, checkpoints, and full training recipe, including a complete specification of the data mixture over publicly available corpora. We hope this release enables the community to study native uniform diffusion at scale and catalyzes work on its as-yet poorly understood aspects.

</details>


### [132] [Which Sections of a Research Paper Best Reveal Its Research Methods? Evidence from Library and Information Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19051)
*Qiuyu Fang,Jiayi Hao,Chengzhi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出按物理位置切分全文并组合片段，用于学术论文研究方法的自动多标签分类；结果表明中后段与末段更具判别力，结合文献元数据和跨片段组合可提升分类效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究方法分类可支持方法检索、综述生成和科研情报分析。既有研究多依赖题名和摘要，但摘要中的方法信息有限；直接使用全文又面临文本过长与冗余信息过多的问题，因此需要更有效地利用全文中的方法相关信息。

Method: 构建来自图书情报学三个代表性期刊 JASIST、LISR 和 JDoc 的 1,954 篇全文标注语料；依据论文全文的物理位置进行分段，设计片段组合策略；在多种模型下比较不同片段及其组合在研究方法多标签分类任务中的表现，并考察文献元数据融合的效果。

Result: 实验发现，研究方法信息在全文中分布不均，中后部片段和末尾片段具有更强区分能力；跨片段组合相比单一片段更有效，加入文献元数据后分类性能进一步提升。

Conclusion: 相较仅使用题名摘要或直接输入全文，基于物理位置的全文分段与组合策略能更精准地捕获方法信息，提升研究方法自动多标签分类效果，对知识服务和科研分析具有应用价值。

Abstract: Research methods are essential carriers of knowledge contribution in academic papers. Automatic multi-label classification of research methods can support knowledge services such as method retrieval, review generation, and research intelligence analysis. While existing studies primarily rely on titles and abstracts, abstracts often provide only limited methodological information, whereas utilizing full-text content faces challenges related to excessive length and information redundancy. Therefore, this paper proposes a segment combination strategy by partitioning the full-text content according to its physical postion. Using an annotated corpus of 1,954 full-text articles from three representative journals in Library and Information Science (JASIST, LISR, and JDoc), we evaluate the classification performance of various segments and their combinations across multiple models. Experimental results indicate that methodological information is distributed unevenly within the full-text content, with the middle-to-late and final segments exhibiting greater discriminative power. Furthermore, integrating bibliographic metadata with cross-segment combination strategies effectively enhances classification performance.

</details>


### [133] [Leadership as Coordination Control: Behavioral Signatures and the Recovery-Advantage Boundary in Multi-Agent LLM Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19111)
*Haewoon Kwak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体大语言模型团队中，过程级协调控制何时真正提升表现；结论是不存在普适最优控制器，只有在初始多数意见不可靠、任务可恢复且普通交互无法自行修复时，控制才有增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 团队科学认为领导作用具有情境依赖性：领导并非总是有效，能力强且自治的团队甚至可能不需要领导。论文将这一问题迁移到多智能体LLM团队，试图回答过程级协调控制在何种可测条件下产生价值，以及这些条件是否符合团队科学中的权变理论预测。

Method: 作者用行为特征衡量团队过程，包括多数锁定、探索行为、从错误的第0轮共识中恢复等，并通过逐动作消融进行分析。论文将三类经典领导风格——交易型、变革型、情境型——操作化为共享动作词表上的控制器，动作包括探索、修订、接受、综合。同时设置使用相同动作但规则任意的匹配控制器，以区分动作词表本身和理论规则的贡献。实验覆盖四类任务机制与三个开源权重模型族。

Result: 结果显示，没有任何控制器在准确率上全面占优，符合权变视角。交易型控制在12个模型—任务组合中与共享第0轮投票结果几乎一致，差距在1.3个百分点以内。显著收益只出现在第0轮多数意见不可靠的一个组合中，即llama-4-scout social设置下，情境型控制比扁平交互高8个百分点。使用相同动作但任意规则的控制器并不优于多数投票，说明有效性来自理论推导的规则而非动作集合。

Conclusion: 论文认为，多智能体LLM团队中的过程级协调控制应被视为一种需要测量和理论映射的权变机制，而不是单纯追求排行榜最优的技术模块。大部分准确率上的空结果并非控制器失败，而是与团队科学中领导替代、路径—目标冗余和情境准备度缺口等理论预测一致。

Abstract: Team science holds that leadership is contingent: it helps only under specific conditions, and capable, autonomous teams may need none at all. We ask the analogous question for multi-agent LLM teams: under what measurable conditions does process-level coordination control add value, and do those conditions match what team science predicts? We use behavioral signatures (majority lock-in, exploration, recovery from an incorrect round-0 consensus) and per-action ablations, clean because each controller is an explicit action set, not a monolithic prompt. We operationalize three classical leadership styles (transactional, transformational, situational) as controllers over a shared action vocabulary (explore, revise, accept, synthesize). A matched controller with the same actions but an arbitrary rule recovers no better than majority voting, so the theory-derived rule, not the vocabulary, does the work. Across four task regimes and three open-weight model families, no controller dominates by accuracy, as the contingency view predicts: transactional control matches a shared round-0 vote on all 12 (model, regime) combinations to within 1.3pp, and gains appear only on the one combination where the round-0 majority is unreliable (llama-4-scout social; situational +8pp over flat). A recovery-advantage account, tested with four boundary probes, says a controller beats plain interaction only where the round-0 majority is unreliable, the task is recoverable, and undirected interaction does not already repair it. These regions map onto contingency theory (leadership substitutes, path-goal redundancy, the situational readiness gap), so a largely null accuracy result is what the theory predicts, not a failure of the controllers. We read process-level coordination control as a contingency to be measured and theory-mapped, not a leaderboard to be topped.

</details>


### [134] [Dango: A Strictly L1-Only Large Language Model for Studying Second Language Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19170)
*Shiho Matta,Yin Jou Huang,Fei Cheng,Takashi Kodama,Hirokazu Kiyomaru,Yugo Murawaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: We introduce Dango, a 1.8B-parameter large language model designed for controlled studies of L1-to-L2 (Japanese-to-English) transfer in second language acquisition (SLA). While previous studies have explored SLA in language models, they have predominantly relied on smaller or non-decoder models, limiting their ability to generate open-ended text and reducing their suitability as practical L2 simulators. We identify a key challenge when scaling models to this size: L2 contamination within the "monolingual" pretraining corpus used for L1 acquisition. To address this, we propose a filtering method to reduce premature exposure to English while preserving realistic, minimal exposure. We then fine-tune the model on LLM-generated L2-learning lessons to simulate the L2 acquisition process. Our evaluations confirm that Dango develops human-like L2 production patterns, outperforming both unfiltered and standard multilingual baselines. We release the model, data, and code to facilitate reproducible computational SLA research and learner-facing applications.

</details>


### [135] [Language Models as Interfaces, Not Oracles: A Hybrid LLM-ML System for Pediatric Appendicitis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19183)
*Soheyl Bateni,Maryam Abdolali*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ClaMPAPP：用大语言模型从临床叙事中抽取结构化特征，再由 XGBoost 进行儿童阑尾炎风险预测；相比端到端 LLM，性能更稳健、漏诊更少、可审计性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 能理解自由文本病历并提升临床决策支持的可用性，但直接作为诊断模型时易受提示词、信息顺序影响，并可能生成看似合理但错误的结论。传统结构化机器学习模型预测更稳定，但依赖表格化输入，难以融入叙事型临床工作流。因此需要一种兼具自然语言可用性与稳定预测能力的混合方案。

Method: 作者构建 ClaMPAPP 混合管线：首先用 LLM 作为接口，从类似病历记录的叙事文本中抽取受预定义 schema 约束的临床特征；随后进行确定性的合理性检查；最后将验证后的临床、实验室和超声特征输入 XGBoost 分类器进行阑尾炎诊断风险预测。实验在两个独立德国医院儿童阑尾炎队列上进行，并与开源及闭源端到端 LLM 基线比较。为在保留真实标签的同时测试自由文本输入，研究将结构化电子病历通过模板渲染和受约束 LLM 改写生成叙事文本，并通过句子顺序扰动评估位置鲁棒性。

Result: ClaMPAPP 在内部验证和外部验证中均取得最佳整体诊断性能，并在急诊分诊中最关键的安全指标——减少阑尾炎漏诊方面表现突出。相比之下，端到端 LLM 的敏感性与特异性权衡不稳定，并且在叙事文本重排序后性能下降更明显。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将 LLM 用作自然语言接口、将机器学习模型用作最终预测器的设计，可以将文本可用性与预测推理相分离，在临床决策支持中提供更稳定、更安全且更可审计的路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can make clinical decision support more accessible by interpreting free-text documentation, but their direct use as diagnostic engines is limited by sensitivity to prompts, information order, and plausible but incorrect outputs. Structured machine-learning models offer more stable risk prediction, yet they require tabular inputs that are difficult to integrate with narrative clinical workflows. We present ClaMPAPP (Clinical Language-assisted Machine-learning Pipeline for Appendicitis), a hybrid system that uses an LLM as an interface rather than as the final decision-maker. ClaMPAPP extracts schema-constrained clinical features from note-like narratives, applies deterministic plausibility checks, and passes validated features to an XGBoost classifier trained on clinical, laboratory, and ultrasound variables. We evaluated ClaMPAPP on two independent pediatric appendicitis cohorts from German hospitals and compared it with end-to-end LLM baselines, including open-source and proprietary models. To preserve ground truth while testing free-text input, narratives were generated from structured electronic health records through template rendering and constrained LLM rewriting, with additional sentence-order permutation to assess positional robustness. ClaMPAPP achieved the strongest overall diagnostic performance in both internal and external validation while minimizing missed appendicitis cases, the key safety concern in acute triage. End-to-end LLMs showed unstable sensitivity-specificity trade-offs and greater degradation under narrative reordering. These results support an LLM-as-interface, ML-as-predictor design that separates natural-language usability from predictive inference and provides a more auditable pathway for clinical decision support.

</details>


### [136] [RECOM: A Validity Discrimination Tradeoff in Automatic Metrics for Open Ended Reddit Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19218)
*Pushwitha Krishnappa,Amit Das,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha,Tathagata Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 RECOM 数据集，用于评估开放式问答中自动指标的有效性与区分能力，发现常用指标难以同时胜任两者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 文本评估常依赖自动指标，但这些指标既要判断生成内容是否真正与参考答案对齐，又要区分不同模型优劣；在开放式、观点驱动问答中，这两项目标可能存在冲突。

Method: 构建包含 15,000 个 r/AskReddit 问题及真实社区回复的无污染数据集 RECOM，并使用五个 7–10B 开源 LLM 生成答案；将多种自动指标对真实回复打分，并引入随机错配回复作为噪声基线，同时控制回答长度并使用多个 LLM judge 作对照。

Result: 余弦相似度能较好区分真实答案与随机答案，但几乎不能区分五个模型；BERTScore precision 表面上能区分模型，但控制长度后效果显著下降，且有效性弱于余弦相似度；LLM judge 也复现了有效性差距且模型区分能力较弱。

Conclusion: 自动指标在开放式问答评估中存在有效性与区分能力的权衡，这种问题源于指标表示设计而非模型本身；建议评估时同时报告有效性和区分能力，并加入明确的随机基线。

Abstract: Automatic metrics are the default for evaluating LLM-generated text, yet a metric is quietly asked to do two jobs: tell genuine content alignment from surface coincidence (validity), and tell a better system from a worse one (discriminative power). On open-ended, opinion-driven question answering, the two are in tension. We introduce RECOM (Reddit Evaluation for Correspondence of Models), a contamination-free evaluation dataset of 15,000 r/AskReddit questions (September 2025), each paired with its authentic community replies, which postdate every evaluated model's training cutoff. Scoring five open-source LLMs (7--10B) against every reply each metric paired with a random-derangement noise floor we find that no metric does both jobs well. Cosine similarity separates real from random answers (Cohen's $d \approx 2$) but cannot rank the five models ($|d| < 0.1$); BERTScore precision appears to rank the models (raw $|d|$ up to 0.63), but once response length is controlled this collapses to $|d| = 0.09$ and its validity is weak ($d \approx 0.8$, versus cosine's $\approx 2$). Because every metric scores the same outputs, this validity--discrimination tradeoff is a property of the metrics, not the models, and we argue it stems from representation design. Three independent LLM judges reproduce the validity gap and likewise separate the five models only weakly. We recommend reporting metrics on both axes, with an explicit random-baseline floor. RECOM is publicly available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/recom-D4B0

</details>


### [137] [DreamReasoner-8B: Block-Size Curriculum Learning for Diffusion Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19257)
*Zirui Wu,Lin Zheng,Jiacheng Ye,Shansan Gong,Xueliang Zhao,Yansong Feng,Wei Bi,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究 DreamReasoner-8B，证明块扩散语言模型可通过合适训练策略支持长链式思维推理，并在数学与代码推理任务上达到与领先开源自回归模型相竞争的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块扩散语言模型能通过并行的块级去噪加速解码，但其是否能够可靠扩展到长 CoT 推理仍不明确；尤其是训练与推理时块大小对推理能力的影响缺乏系统研究。

Method: 作者开发开源模型 DreamReasoner-8B，系统评估训练块大小和推理块大小对长 CoT 推理的影响；发现大块训练损害推理能力后，提出块大小课程学习，从细粒度小块逐步过渡到粗粒度大块，以弥合训练粒度差距。

Result: 实验表明，直接使用大块训练会导致推理性能显著下降，而小块训练能保持有效推理；采用块大小课程学习后，模型在不同推理块大小下均具备较强泛化能力，并在数学和代码推理基准上取得与 Qwen3-8B 等领先开源自回归模型相竞争的结果。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过块大小课程学习，块扩散语言模型可以兼顾高效解码与强推理能力，为构建高效、具备长 CoT 推理能力的扩散语言模型提供了实践基础。

Abstract: Block diffusion language models accelerate decoding through parallel block-wise denoising, yet whether they can be reliably scaled for long chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning remains unresolved. To this end, we develop DreamReasoner-8B, an open-source block diffusion reasoning model, and conduct a systematic study of how training and inference block sizes affect long-CoT reasoning. Our analysis reveals a stark performance disparity: training with large block sizes yields remarkably poor reasoning, whereas small block sizes preserve effective reasoning. To bridge this granularity gap, we propose block-size curriculum learning, which gradually transitions training from fine-grained to coarse-grained block sizes, thereby overcoming this limitation and enabling strong reasoning performance that generalizes across diverse inference block sizes. On mathematical and code reasoning benchmarks, DreamReasoner-8B achieves results competitive with leading open autoregressive models such as Qwen3-8B. This work establishes a practical foundation for efficient, reasoning-capable diffusion language models. We release our model at https://github.com/DreamLM/DreamReasoner.

</details>


### [138] [Trade-offs in Medical LLM Adaptation: An Empirical Study in French QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19266)
*Ikram Belmadani,Oumaima El Khettari,Carlos Ramisch,Frederic Bechet,Richard Dufour,Benoit Favre*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文以法语医学问答为案例，系统比较大语言模型医学领域适配策略，发现SFT在选择题任务中通常是高性价比默认方案，而CPT对开放式问答的词面指标更有帮助；CPT+SFT在部分场景最佳但增益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在专业领域和特定语言中的适配需求不断增加，但不同领域适配策略（如持续预训练、监督微调及其组合）的实际效果、成本收益和适用场景仍不清晰，尤其缺乏对基座模型选择与适配效果的解耦分析。

Method: 研究以法语医学QA为测试场景，比较CPT、SFT、CPT+SFT三类策略，覆盖三个模型家族、多种模型规模和三种初始化方式；在多项选择问答与开放式问答上评估，并使用贪婪解码、约束解码、自动指标和LLM-as-a-Judge进行综合评价，同时进行跨语言迁移实验。

Result: 在MCQA中，CPT+SFT多数情况下取得最高分，但相较SFT的提升较小且经常不具统计显著性，因此SFT更具成本效益；在OEQA中，CPT稳定提升基于重叠的自动指标，而SFT常降低生成质量；LLM评审更偏好指令调优和CPT+SFT。法语适配还可有效迁移到英语基准。

Conclusion: 论文给出面向计算资源约束的实践建议：选择题医学QA中优先考虑SFT作为强基线和低成本方案；开放式QA中CPT或CPT+SFT更值得考虑；适配策略选择应结合任务形式、评估方式、模型基座与预算综合决策。

Abstract: The development of large language models (LLMs) has led to an increased focus on their adaptation to specialized domains and languages, yet the effectiveness of domain adaptation strategies remains unclear. We present a study of medical domain adaptation using French medical question-answering (QA) as a case study. We compare continual pretraining (CPT), supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and their combination across three model families, multiple sizes, and three initialization types, explicitly disentangling adaptation effects from base model choice. We evaluate both multiple-choice (MCQA) and open-ended QA (OEQA) under greedy and constrained decoding using automatic metrics and LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation. For MCQA, CPT+SFT most often achieves the best scores, but gains over SFT are small and frequently not statistically significant, making SFT a strong and cost-effective default. For OEQA, CPT consistently improves overlap-based metrics, while SFT often degrades generation quality; instruction tuning and CPT+SFT are preferred by LLM-based evaluation. Cross-lingual experiments further show effective transfer from French adaptation to English benchmarks. Overall, we provide practical guidelines for selecting adaptation strategies under computational constraints.

</details>


### [139] [Enhancing Decision-Making with Large Language Models through Multi-Agent Fictitious Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19308)
*Leyang Shen,Yang Zhang,Xiaoyan Zhao,Chun Kai Ling,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MAFP，将利益相关方立场建模为智能体，并用虚拟博弈的均衡搜索过程解决多方决策中的“立场纠缠”问题，在策略决策任务上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的多智能体系统擅长通过分解子任务处理执行复杂性，但对真实场景中多方决策相互依赖的问题效果不足；这类任务需要同时考虑所有利益相关方立场，不能简单隔离求解。

Method: 作者提出 Multi-Agent Fictitious Play（MAFP）：将不同利益相关方的立场表示为智能体，把决策过程形式化为寻求均衡的博弈过程。每轮中，每个智能体根据其他智能体历史决策的经验混合分布进行最佳响应，迭代更新自身决策。

Result: 在具有挑战性的决策任务中，MAFP 在 tournament strength 和 robustness 两项互补指标上均超过单轮和多轮基线方法。

Conclusion: MAFP 能有效处理多方决策中的立场纠缠，通过迭代博弈式交互提升决策质量与鲁棒性，为 LLM 多智能体系统从执行分解扩展到复杂决策提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have demonstrated great potential in solving tasks with execution complexity, by distributing subtasks across cooperative agents. However, this divide-and-conquer paradigm falls short on decision-making tasks that are also prevalent in the real world. These tasks require simultaneous reasoning from the stances of all involved stakeholders whose decisions are mutually dependent and thus cannot be solved in isolation. We characterize this challenge as stance entanglement, a form of decision complexity distinct from execution complexity. To address it, we propose Multi-Agent Fictitious Play (MAFP), a novel MAS paradigm that represents stakeholder stances as agents and formulates decision-making as an equilibrium-seeking process. Built on the game-theoretic principle of fictitious play, MAFP iteratively updates each agent's decision by best responding to the empirical mixture of other agents' past decisions. This enables agents to expose and address one another's weaknesses, progressively improving decision quality and robustness. We evaluate MAFP on challenging decision-making tasks that test the capability of deciding strategies for competitive scenarios prior to acting. MAFP outperforms both single-round and multi-round baselines on two complementary metrics, tournament strength and robustness, demonstrating its effectiveness in addressing stance entanglement.

</details>


### [140] [Freeing the Law with LOCUS: A Local Ordinance Corpus for the United States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19334)
*Denis Peskoff,Joe Barrow,Christopher Vu,Diag Davenport*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍 LOCUS：一个面向美国地方条例的机器可读大规模语料库及县级协调访问层，并提供覆盖元数据与基于 ModernBERT 的分析模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律 AI 研究缺乏大规模、权威且机器可读的地方条例文本；地方条例覆盖日常监管的多个领域，但分散在面向人工浏览的供应商平台和多种文档格式中，难以批量获取与分析。

Method: 作者收集几乎所有公开可得的市政与县级条例代码，使用 OCR 处理多样化文档格式；构建原始语料与县级协调访问层，发布覆盖元数据；并训练一组基于 ModernBERT 的分类器和评分器，用于分析地方性法律文本的多个维度。

Result: 原始语料包含来自 9,239 个城市和县的条例代码；县级协调的 LOCUS 访问层覆盖 3,144 个美国县中最大的 2,309 个，覆盖多数人口；数据集及衍生模型已在 Hugging Face 发布。

Conclusion: LOCUS 为地方条例的可复现研究、法律 AI 下游任务以及地方性法律机器可读访问的扩展提供了基础设施，并使以往难以大规模研究的文本属性分析成为可能。

Abstract: Progress in legal AI increasingly depends on access to authoritative legal text at scale. Yet one of the most consequential layers of American law remains largely absent from existing machine-readable corpora: local ordinances. Local codes govern zoning, housing, business licensing, public health, noise, animal control, and many other domains of everyday regulation, but they are fragmented across vendor platforms designed for human browsing rather than bulk research access. We introduce LOCUS - the Local Ordinance Corpus for the United States - a comprehensive corpus and county-harmonized access layer for U.S. municipal and county ordinance codes. The raw corpus, available for release to researchers, represents nearly all publicly available municipal and county ordinance codes. The resulting raw corpus contains codes from 9,239 cities and counties. A smaller county-harmonized LOCUS access layer provides coverage for the largest 2,309 of 3,144 U.S. counties, accounting for a majority of the population. We use OCR to handle the myriad of document formats that have kept the law from being a public resource. We release the corpus with coverage metadata to support reproducibility, downstream legal AI research, and the incremental expansion of machine-readable access to local law. We train a collection of ModernBERT-based classifiers and scorers to facilitate analyzing U.S. local law among several dimensions, such as opacity and paternalism, that have not previously been studied at this scale. LOCUS-v1 and its derivative models are available at: https://huggingface.co/datasets/LocalLaws/LOCUS-v1

</details>


### [141] [Learning User Simulators with Turing Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19336)
*Yingshan Susan Wang,Cedegao E. Zhang,Linlu Qiu,Zexue He,Pengyuan Li,Alex Pentland,Roger P. Levy,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Turing-RL：一种基于图灵测试奖励的强化学习方法，用于训练更像真实用户的用户模拟器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户模拟器训练通常以单一真实回复为目标，通过最大化似然或相似度奖励进行“答案匹配”，但真实用户在同一上下文下可能有多种合理表达，单一匹配目标限制了模拟质量。

Method: 方法使用 LLM 评审器构造判别式图灵奖励：在给定用户历史的条件下，判断生成回复与真实用户回复的不可区分程度。用户模拟器 LLM 通过强化学习优化该奖励，从而学习生成更难与真实用户区分的回复。

Result: 在两个交互文本场景——对话聊天与论坛讨论——中，Turing-RL 在 LLM 自动评估和人工评估指标上均持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 研究表明，相比直接匹配单个参考回复，优化“与真实用户不可区分性”更适合训练高质量用户模拟器。

Abstract: Learning to simulate human users in interactive settings could advance the training of agent assistants, evaluation of personalization systems, research in the social sciences, and more. Existing approaches generally do so by training a large language model (LLM) to match a single ground truth response, either by maximizing the log probability or by using a similarity reward. We instead propose {Turing-RL}: a Turing-Test-based reinforcement learning approach for training user simulator models. {Turing-RL} uses a discriminative Turing reward with an LLM judge to score how indistinguishable a generated response is from the real user's given the user's history, and the user simulator LLM learns to produce responses indistinguishable from what the user could have said with such rewards. Across two different domains--conversational chat and Reddit forum discussion--we find that {Turing-RL} consistently outperforms baseline methods on both LLM and human evaluation metrics. Our study suggests that optimizing for indistinguishability, rather than response matching, is effective for learning user simulators.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [A skew polynomial framework for constructing division algebras and linear maximum rank distance codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18371)
*Susanne Pumpluen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文利用域上的斜多项式构造非幺除代数与线性最大秩距离（MRD）矩阵码，统一并推广了多类已知预半域/半域与MRD码构造，并给出判别准则与不变量分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的扭循环预半域、广义扭域以及相关线性MRD码构造彼此关联但形式分散；论文旨在通过斜多项式框架建立统一推广，并澄清不同文献中出现的同位除代数之间的关系。

Method: 在域上的斜多项式环中构造非幺除代数和线性矩阵码；推导这些代数成为除代数、对应码达到最大秩距离的判别条件；进一步比较同位类并计算若干结构不变量。

Result: 所得非幺除代数推广了Sheekey扭循环预半域、Jha-Johnson相关预半域以及Albert广义扭域；所得线性MRD码推广了Lobillo、Santonastaso与Sheekey的构造；论文还给出了相关同位结构的比较与不变量计算。

Conclusion: 斜多项式提供了一个统一而有效的代数框架，可同时生成和分析新的除代数与线性MRD码，并将多个经典与近期构造纳入同一理论体系。

Abstract: We construct division algebras and linear maximum rank distance (MRD) matrix codes using skew polynomials over fields. The non-unital division algebras we obtain generalize several prominent constructions: Sheekey's twisted cyclic pre-semifields, i.e. the pre-semifields associated with Jha-Johnson semifields and the semifields associated with Albert's generalized twisted fields. Our linear MRD codes generalize the constructions of Lobillo, Santonastaso and Sheekey. We present criteria for these algebras to be division algebras, respectively, for when these codes have maximum rank, and compare isotopic division algebras that appear throughout recent and classical literature. We compute some of their invariants.

</details>


### [143] [Holographic Integrated Sensing and Communication With Limited Radiation Amplitudes: How Many Quantization Bits Are Enough?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18456)
*Shuhao Zeng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究可重构全息表面（RHS）在全息通感一体化（ISAC）系统中，有限辐射幅度量化对通信速率与感知 SINR 的影响，并给出量化比特数的理论上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全息 ISAC 研究通常假设 RHS 单元能够连续控制辐射信号幅度，但该假设在实际硬件中难以实现。因此，需要分析有限幅度量化条件下系统性能如何退化，以及需要多少量化比特才能满足通信与感知性能要求。

Method: 作者首先推导通信速率与感知 SINR 的闭式下界，用于刻画有限幅度量化带来的性能影响；随后基于这些下界建立最小辐射幅度量化比特数的紧上界，并进一步分析通信-感知性能权衡对所需量化比特数的影响。

Result: 数值仿真验证了理论分析的准确性，表明所推导的性能界和量化比特数上界能够有效预测有限幅度分辨率对全息 ISAC 系统性能的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作说明 RHS 幅度控制的有限分辨率是全息 ISAC 实用化中的关键因素，并为实际系统设计中选择合适的辐射幅度量化比特数提供了理论依据。

Abstract: As a promising solution for extremely large-scale arrays, reconfigurable holographic surfaces (RHS) can be integrated with integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) to form the holographic ISAC paradigm, where significant beamforming gains provided by RHS can improve both communication and sensing performance. However, most existing works on holographic ISAC assume that RHS elements can control the amplitudes of radiated ISAC signals in a continuous manner, which is hard to implement in practice. In this paper, we investigate how the limited radiation amplitudes of the RHS influence ISAC system performance. Specifically, we first derive closed-form lower bounds for both communication rate and sensing SINR to establish a tight upper bound on the minimum radiation amplitude quantization bits. Based on this, we further explore how the communication-sensing performance tradeoff impacts the quantization bits. Numerical results validate our theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [144] [Covert Blockwise Coding with Sequential Detection over Thermal-Loss Bosonic Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18666)
*Qipeng Qian,Yuntao Qian*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出首个面向接收端的、分块序贯检测框架，用于热损耗玻色信道上的隐蔽通信，并给出检测段最小长度的设计准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐蔽量子/光通信研究多偏信息论极限，较少考虑物理可实现接收机下，接收端如何在一个信号块结束前可靠检测活跃块，同时仍满足对监听者的隐蔽性约束。

Method: 将每个块建模为二进制超符号；针对固定的物理可实现广义外差/同相类接收机，分析小信号区域中 Bob 的变点后信息增长与 Willie 的量子相对熵可检测性；在每块隐蔽预算约束下优化检测段内信号分配，并采用单次 CUSUM 序贯检测器推导阈值穿越条件。

Result: 证明 Bob 的后变点信息增长在小信号 regime 下呈线性，而 Willie 的可检测性服从二次量子相对熵律；利用该不对称性，得到检测段内均匀信令是渐近最优策略，并给出 CUSUM 在同一块内以指数高概率越过阈值的显式最小长度条件。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了玻色隐蔽通信、序贯检测与分块信令设计之间的具体联系，为具有物理可实现接收机的隐蔽量子/光通信系统提供了可实施的接收机感知设计准则。

Abstract: We develop, to our knowledge, the first receiver-centric blockwise sequential-detection framework for covert communication over thermal-loss bosonic channels. In this architecture, each block serves as a binary super-symbol, and the key design problem is to determine the minimum detection-segment length that enables Bob to detect an active block before the block ends while remaining covert to Willie. For any fixed physically realizable general-dyne receiver, Bob's post-change information growth is linear in the small-signal regime, whereas Willie's detectability obeys a quadratic quantum relative entropy law. Exploiting this asymmetry, we show that under a per-block covertness budget the asymptotically optimal signaling strategy is uniform across the detection segment, and we derive an explicit minimum-length condition under which a single-pass cumulative sum (CUSUM) detector crosses threshold within the same block with exponentially high probability. The resulting design law yields a covert blockwise binary codebook over a finite transmission horizon and establishes a concrete link between bosonic covert communication, sequential detection, and blockwise signaling design. More broadly, these results provide design guidance for covert quantum communication systems with physically realizable receivers, and help bridge information-theoretic covertness guarantees with implementable receiver-aware optical communication design.

</details>


### [145] [Environment-Aware Resource Allocation for Pinching-Antenna-Assisted EDMA-NOMA Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18899)
*Yaxuan Luo*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究环境地图感知的EDMA-NOMA资源分配，通过联合用户匹配与功率分配提升系统吞吐量与公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EDMA可利用传播环境中的视距可用性、遮挡多样性与空间隔离来调控区域间干扰，NOMA可通过功率域复用和SIC提升区域内接入效率；但若仅假设环境遮挡抑制干扰，会高估系统性能，因此需要更真实地建模环境地图对服务链路和干扰链路的共同影响。

Method: 基于大尺度路径损耗和指数型LoS遮挡模型，构建平均LoS/NLoS有效大尺度信道增益，使环境地图同时作用于期望服务链路与区域间干扰链路；提出效用驱动的联合用户配对与功率分配算法UBA-JPPA，在NOMA功率约束和与SIC一致的速率定义下，同时考虑系统吞吐量与调度用户Jain公平指数。

Result: 仿真表明，相比不使用异构环境地图的消融基线，所提方案在总速率、公平性以及吞吐量—公平性权衡区域上均获得稳定增益；同时，强EDMA-OMA基准在部分高SNR场景中仍具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 异构环境信息能够有效改进EDMA-NOMA系统中的用户匹配与功率分配，但结果并不支持NOMA在所有场景下普遍优于OMA，论文重点在于验证环境地图信息对资源分配的价值。

Abstract: Environment division multiple access (EDMA) exploits line-of-sight (LoS) availability, blockage diversity, and spatial isolation in the propagation environment to regulate inter-region interference, while non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA) improves intra-region access efficiency through power-domain multiplexing and successive interference cancellation (SIC). This paper investigates heterogeneous environment-map-aware user matching and power allocation for pinching-antenna-assisted EDMA-NOMA systems. Based on large-scale path loss and an exponential LoS blockage model, an average LoS/NLoS effective large-scale channel gain is constructed, allowing the environment map to affect both desired service links and inter-region interference links. This avoids the overly optimistic assumption that environmental blockage only suppresses interference. A utility-based joint user-pairing and power-allocation algorithm (UBA-JPPA) is then proposed to jointly account for system throughput and the scheduled-user Jain fairness index under NOMA power constraints and an SIC-consistent rate definition.
